- **hubspot**: Use for any HubSpot request — contacts, companies, deals, tickets, engagements, marketing email statistics, or traffic analytics. Every HubSpot "report" is extract-then-aggregate-locally, which collides with a hard 10,000-result cap on Search. Plan the shard before starting.
